# Gap table: bee

Made by `tools/views/gaps.py`. The 1200 species most recorded worldwide that borrow this view cover 93.7% of its 2,515,754 research-grade records; the 20 most recorded in Alachua County cover 67.3% of the county's 4,245. Counts are iNaturalist research-grade records through 2024. The view's own measured animal is not excluded: it shows as `m` where it was measured.

Row grades: `m` every trait of the row measured in that species; `g` every trait measured or carried by a rule; `i` at least one trait neither. The detail columns give the state of each trait.

Rows and their traits:

- **Sharpness**: acuity_cpd, interommatidial_deg
- **Red**: lambda_max_nm, receptor_classes
- **Speed**: cff_hz, corner_hz
- **Ultraviolet**: lambda_max_nm, spectral_range_nm
- **How the colours are shown**: receptor_classes, colour_vision
